# Work packages

Generated by the ECC planner on 2026-09-23 from the code-architect designs (`designs.md`) and the live firm rechecks (`live-recheck.md`). Each package runs under ECC orch-fix-defect: a failing regression test first, then the fix to green, then review. Packages in the same wave own disjoint files.


## WP01: CLI flag readers: bounded numeric flags, --trials, --stop, --ladder and comma-list parsing, --max-lifetime-payouts, --path-granularity

- **Wave:** 1  **Size:** large  **Depends on:** none
- **Items:** R1-23, R1-24, R1-25, R1-27, TG-3, TG-5, TG-6
- **Files owned:** `src/cli/commands/prop/shared.ts`, `src/cli/commands/prop/optimize/dp/command.ts`, `src/cli/commands/prop/optimize/funded/command.ts`, `tests/unit/cli/prop/shared.test.ts`, `tests/unit/cli/prop/optimizeDp.test.ts`, `tests/unit/cli/prop/optimizeFunded.test.ts`

**Steps**

1. Run under ECC orch-fix-defect (ecc:tdd-guide drives RED/GREEN, ecc:typescript-reviewer reviews at the end). Edit only files_owned. The live, ladder and sim reader swaps are done by WP09, WP10 and WP06/WP11 against the readers added here; the R1-23 header lines (describeStopRule) land in WP06 (sim) and WP10 (ladder). No code comments, ~/ imports, no git commits (the user commits).
2. Write RED tests in tests/unit/cli/prop/shared.test.ts. (a) describe('flag bounds'): TradingInputs.parse(parseArgs([...], ARGS)) throws naming the flag for --winrate 40 (message contains 'fraction in [0, 1]'), 1.01, -0.1; --eval-discount 150 and -5; --activation-discount 200; --monthly-discount 101; --idle-day-probability 5; --risk -250 and 0; --funded-risk 0; --rr 0; --tpd 2.5; --copy-accounts 0; --max-attempts 0; --eval-days 0; --funded-days -1; --commission -1; --retain-cushion -1; --seed 1.5; --request-size 0; --stop-points 0. Boundaries accepted through toSimInputs(apex eod plan): winrate 0 and 1, eval-discount 100, all discounts 0 gives discounts undefined, idle 1. readFraction(true, 'idle-day-probability') throws; readPositiveNumber('', 'risk') and ('Infinity', 'risk') throw; readPercentAsFraction('5', 'x') === 0.05.
3. (b) R1-25: --trials 0, -1, 0.5, abc throw /--trials must be a whole number >= 1/; '1' gives trials 1; default gives 4000.
4. (c) R1-23/TG-3 describe('--stop parsing (readStopRule)'): accepts after-target:500, after-target:$500, after-target:1250.5, bare after-target (500), after-k-losses:3, bare after-k-losses (2), day-green, first-win, none. Rejects with a message containing '--stop' and the raw value: after-target:abc, after-target:, after-target:$, after-target:00 (message also contains 'single-quote'), after-target:-100, after-target:500:1, after-k-losses:notanumber, after-k-losses:0, after-k-losses:1.5, day-green:5, bogus. Wiring: --stop 'after-target:$500' reaches SimInputs.dayStop {kind AfterTarget, dollars 500}. describeStopRule(readStopRule(form)) round-trips none, day-green, first-win, after-target:$500, after-k-losses:2.
5. (d) R1-27 describe('--ladder parsing'): readLadder throws /entry 2 is empty/ for '400,,600' and '400, ,600', /is empty/ for ',400,600' and '400,600,', /rung 3 follows a \$0 rung/ for '400,0,600', /first rung must be > 0/ for '0' and '0,400', throws for '400,-1' and '400,abc'; accepts '400,600', ' 400 , 600 ', '400,600,0'; undefined and '' give null; readLadder('400,,600','funded-ladder') message names --funded-ladder. readNumberList('1,2','x',z.number(),'n') gives [1,2]; '1,,2' throws /--x.*entry 2 is empty/.
6. (e) TG-5: readMaxLifetimePayouts undefined and '' give undefined; unlimited, none, UNLIMITED, None give null; '3' gives 3; '0', '-1', '2.5', 'abc' throw /--max-lifetime-payouts/; wiring on E8 signature (unlimited gives plan.maxLifetimePayouts null, '3' gives 3, omitted keeps plan identity); apex eod with unlimited keeps payoutLadder.capsAtLastStep true. (f) TG-6: readGranularityList(String(MAX_PATH_GRANULARITY)) gives [MAX], MAX+1, '0', '-4', '2.5', 'abc', '4,,25', '4,25,' throw; ' 4, 10 ,25' gives [4,10,25]; wiring '10,4' gives intradayPathStepsPerR 10 and inputs list [10,4].
7. Add RED cases to tests/unit/cli/prop/optimizeFunded.test.ts (readNumberList('150,,200','flat', z.number().positive(), ...) throws /--flat/; '5,150' as --percent throws /--percent/) and tests/unit/cli/prop/optimizeDp.test.ts (dp args --winrate 40, --trials 0, --seed 1.5 each throw naming the flag).
8. Confirm RED: bunx vitest run tests/unit/cli/prop/shared.test.ts tests/unit/cli/prop/optimizeDp.test.ts tests/unit/cli/prop/optimizeFunded.test.ts. Record that failures are missing exports or silent acceptance (NaN stop, 0 rung, accepted out-of-range values), not unrelated errors.
9. Implement in shared.ts: private numericFlagSchema = z.union([z.number(), z.string().trim().min(1).pipe(z.coerce.number())]) and parseFlag(schema, raw, name, expectation) throwing `--<name> must be <expectation>, got "<raw>"`. Export readFraction (fractionSchema), readPercent (percentSchema), readPercentAsFraction, readPositiveNumber, readNonNegativeNumber, readPositiveInteger, readInteger, readNumberList<T>, readLadder(raw, name = 'ladder') with the leading-zero and zero-then-positive guards, readGranularityList on readNumberList with z.number().int().positive().max(MAX_PATH_GRANULARITY), export MAX_PATH_GRANULARITY, readMaxLifetimePayouts (trimmed case-insensitive sentinels, readPositiveInteger), readRebuyLagDays via readNonNegativeNumber, readStopRule (split on first colon, z.enum(DayStopRuleKind), exhaustive switch, per-kind schemas) and describeStopRule. Update the --stop and --winrate help text (no em dashes).
10. TradingInputs.parse mapping: fraction for winrate and idle-day-probability; percent for the three discount flags; positive number for risk, funded-risk, rr, funded-rr, request-size, stop-points; positive integer for trials, tpd, funded-tpd, max-attempts, copy-accounts, eval-days, funded-days; non-negative for commission, retain-cushion; integer for seed. Brand TradingInputs field types (Fraction0to1, Percent0to100) and drop the percent() casts in toSimInputs.
11. Keep readNumber exported for now but re-implement it on numericFlagSchema so it rejects booleans and '' (closes the silent R1-18 path immediately). live/command.ts and ladder/command.ts still call it until WP09 and WP10 migrate; WP11 deletes it.
12. optimize/dp/command.ts: readFraction for winrate (drop fraction()), readPositiveNumber for rr, readPositiveInteger for eval-days, funded-days, trials, iterations, readInteger for seed. optimize/funded/command.ts: delete readCandidateList, use readNumberList for --flat (positive) and --percent (positive, max 100), readLadder(..., 'funded-ladder').
13. GREEN: rerun the three test files. Then bun run typecheck and bun run lint. Rerun tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/rebuyLag.test.ts as a guard.
14. Report: inputs now rejected (fractional --tpd, --copy-accounts 1.5, --funded-days 0, --max-attempts 0, --max-lifetime-payouts 0, mid-ladder zero rungs, after-target:0). Surface as open decisions to the user: whether --max-lifetime-payouts 0, --funded-days 0 and '400,0,600' should stay legal. State confirmed vs unverified explicitly.

**Regression tests**

- tests/unit/cli/prop/shared.test.ts: describe('flag bounds'), describe('--stop parsing (readStopRule)'), describe('--ladder parsing'), readNumberList, readMaxLifetimePayouts, readGranularityList, --trials cases
- tests/unit/cli/prop/optimizeFunded.test.ts: --flat/--percent/--funded-ladder list parsing
- tests/unit/cli/prop/optimizeDp.test.ts: dp flag bounds
- guard: tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/rebuyLag.test.ts and existing --rebuy-lag-days tests unchanged

**Acceptance**

- Every numeric prop flag in sim/compare/optimize parses through a Zod reader with its documented bound; out-of-range, boolean, empty, NaN and Infinity inputs exit with `--<flag> must be ...`
- --stop after-target:$500 yields dollars 500; malformed stop args throw instead of silently disabling the rule
- '400,,600' and a positive rung after a $0 rung throw; readCandidateList is gone (one comma-list parser)
- readMaxLifetimePayouts, readGranularityList, MAX_PATH_GRANULARITY, readStopRule, describeStopRule are exported and tested
- bun run typecheck and bun run lint pass; owned tests green

## WP02: Intraday path-walk truncation resolved from the exact gambler's-ruin law

- **Wave:** 1  **Size:** standard  **Depends on:** none
- **Items:** R1-28
- **Files owned:** `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/TradePathSimulation.ts`, `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/TradePathSimulation.test.ts`

**Steps**

1. Run under ECC orch-fix-defect. Decision: MAX_INTRADAY_PATH_STEPS stays in simulator/day.ts because the WP15 ladder rewrite does not call simulateTradePath, so no day.ts or barrel edit here.
2. RED tests in TradePathSimulation.test.ts: replace the 'force-resolves toward the nearer barrier' test with simulateTradePath(0.5, 10, 2, scripted [0.99,0.99,0.99,0.1], 3) giving win, peakR 2, steps 3 (exact win chance 7/30). Add the losing-peak test simulateTradePath(0.5, 10, 2, scripted [0,0,0,0.5,0.5], 3) giving loss, peakR close to 0.8 (scripted rng throws on exhaustion to pin draw count). Add: winrate preserved at maxSteps 1 (p = calibrateStepProbability(0.4, 3, 10), mulberry32(2024), 100k trials, win fraction toBeCloseTo(0.4, 2)); maxSteps 50 with >50% truncated still 0.4; losing-peak mean and P(peakR >= 1) invariant between maxSteps 1 and 100000 within 0.01; win peakR === rrRatio and loss peakR in [0, rrRatio); draw count equals steps for resolved paths and cap+1 (win) / cap+2 (loss) when truncated; p = 0 and p = 1 at the cap produce no NaN.
3. Confirm RED with bunx vitest run tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/TradePathSimulation.test.ts (the current code returns loss and peak 0.3).
4. Implement: rename stepUpProbabilityToWinChance to winProbabilityFrom(p, distanceToLoss, distanceToWin) with the formula unchanged (calibrated p stays bit-identical); add lossProbabilityFrom by symmetry; sampleLosingPeak via binary search on the monotone survival function G(m); resolveTruncatedPath that throws (naming simulateTradePath) if the win chance is non-finite or outside [0,1]; replace the nearer-barrier block. Public signature and TradePathResult unchanged.
5. GREEN, then bun run typecheck, bun run lint. Guards: tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/IntradayPathDrawdown.test.ts, combinatorialCoverage.test.ts, rngDrawCount.test.ts must be unchanged (seeded outputs identical for runs that never hit the cap).
6. CLI observation (CLI only, no scripts): bun run cli prop sim --firm apex --variant intraday --rr 3 --trials 300 --path-granularity 25,50,100 must print the same rows as before the change (seed stability). Optionally add 200 and check it is within noise of the 100 row; report timing honestly if skipped.
7. Report that bias is removed but per-trade cost is unchanged; the cap-lowering and closed-form follow-ups are open questions.

**Regression tests**

- tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/TradePathSimulation.test.ts: truncated win from exact probability, losing-peak sampling, winrate preserved under truncation, peak invariance, draw-count contract, p = 0/1 at cap
- guards: IntradayPathDrawdown.test.ts, combinatorialCoverage.test.ts, rngDrawCount.test.ts unchanged

**Acceptance**

- A truncated path resolves win/loss with the exact absorption probability and samples the losing peak from its conditional law
- Calibrated winrate is preserved at any maxSteps >= 1
- Seeded outputs are byte-identical for runs that never reach the cap
- typecheck, lint and the listed tests pass

- **R1-1 bundle discount, exact rule (user-pasted Tradeify Pricing Reference, 2026-09-23; see live-recheck.md):**
  - It applies only to Growth 25K/50K and Select 25K/50K; 100K, 150K and Lightning are excluded.
  - Only whole bundles of exactly 5 same type and size accounts are discounted, so discounted copies = 5 x floor(n / 5); the rest are bought singly at full price.
  - Re-buys and resets after a failure are separate later orders at full price ("won't apply retroactively to a later order").
  - It stacks multiplicatively with a promo code (example: $545 x 0.95 = $517, then 40% off, about $310).
- **Extra files owned:** `src/lib/prop-calculator/firms/lucid/LucidTrading.ts` and its tests; `src/lib/prop-calculator/portfolioTimeline/*.ts` and `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/portfolioTimeline*`; `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/RenewalCycleObjective.ts` and its tests; `src/cli/commands/prop/optimize/funded/command.ts` and `tests/unit/cli/prop/optimizeFunded.test.ts`; web files `src/app/(app)/prop-calculator/_components/` StrategyAnalysis, StrategyLabPanel, ResultsPanel, TradingInputs, PortfolioPanel and CashFlowPanel (plus their hooks), with new pure helper `.ts` files and tests under `tests/unit/app/prop-calculator/`; the guard tests `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/combinatorialCoverage*.test.ts` and `simulator*.test.ts` that WP06 named. Do not touch live files (`LivePlan.ts`, `livePhase.ts`, `*Live.ts`), which WP12b owns in parallel.

**Steps**

1. Run under ECC orch-fix-defect. Chosen D1 contract (engine-aggregates cluster owns it; the ladder adapts in WP15): replacementEconomics({ discounts, evalPassRate, fees, meanDaysOnFail, meanDaysOnPass }) where evalPassRate is per eval attempt; cost = initialEvalFee + (1/p - 1) * retryFee + subscriptionFee(daysPerFundedAccount) + activationFee once; retryFee = min(reset, re-buy); throws for p outside [0,1] or non-finite; Infinity at p <= 0. One TradingInputs.toCouponDiscounts() accessor (no second discounts getter). D3 wording comes from core PLAN_AVAILABILITY_LABEL (no shared.ts describePlanAvailability). Order: R1-2, R1-1, R1-34.

## WP17: Funded DP: within-day daily loss limit, per-day-start solve, peak ratchet dimension (DP half of R1-43/R1-54), cycle-baseline state for payouts

- **Wave:** 8  **Size:** large  **Depends on:** WP04, WP13, WP15, WP16

Leave those lines and that field alone, re-read `EvalStateValue.ts` before each edit, and keep the field through any refactor. Everything else in `EvalStateValue.ts` is yours.
- **Extra files owned (added 2026-09-23):** `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/ContractLimits.ts`, `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/FundedPayoutCycle.ts`, `src/lib/prop-calculator/firms/apex/ApexTraderFunding.ts` (notes only: the WP04b handoff, the DP now enforces the Apex PA Level DLL on the session-open basis), `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/ContractLimits.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/FundedPayoutCycle.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/PlanUniversalInvariants.test.ts`.
- **N-8 (eval DP):** day-start states use `peakDayCloseProfit` 0, so next-session tiered eval DLLs stay on their lowest tier; thread the same ratchet dimension into the eval DP (or prove no shipped eval DLL is peak-based and pin that with a test). **N-9:** the eval DP risk cap at the DLL headroom (this is R1-6 step 6 below). **N-10:** funded plans with `minQualifyingDayProfit` but no funded consistency count qualifying days from the day's P&L, not the end-of-day cushion (R1-6's todayPnL fix; pin it).
- **T11:** measure, report before/after timings and state counts, and put the `cycleBaselineFineRangeMultiple` default to the user in your report (do not block on it; land the exact default if its measured cost is under 2x, otherwise land 1 and report the options).
- **Files owned:** `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/FundedStateValue.ts`, `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/FundedCycleBaselineGrid.ts`, `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/EvalStateValue.ts`, `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/Plan.ts`, `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/DayPolicy.ts`, `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/DailyLossLimit.ts`, `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/DrawdownStrategy.ts`, `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/index.ts`, `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/LadderSearch.ts`, `src/lib/prop-calculator/simulator/day.ts`, `src/lib/prop-calculator/simulator/types.ts`, `src/lib/prop-calculator/simulator/fundedPhase.ts`, `src/cli/commands/prop/optimize/dp/command.ts`, `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/FundedStateValue.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/FundedCycleBaselineGrid.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/EvalStateValue.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/Plan.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/DayPolicy.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/DailyLossLimit.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/DrawdownStrategy.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/simulator/fundedPhase.test.ts`, `tests/unit/cli/prop/optimizeDp.test.ts`

## WP17b: renewal-cycle objective prices a failed eval at the cheaper retry (DP half of N-12 and R1-2)

- **Wave:** 8 (runs alongside WP17)  **Size:** medium  **Depends on:** WP14
- **Items:** N-12 (DP half), R1-2 (DP half)
- **Files owned:** `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/RenewalCycleObjective.ts`, `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/AverageRewardSolver.ts`, `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/RenewalCycleObjective.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/AverageRewardSolver.test.ts`, and ONLY this narrow part of `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/EvalStateValue.ts`: one optional config field `terminalValueAtFail?: number` (default 0) on its config interface and its use in the eval failure terminal returns (today `return 0` on an eval bust, and on any other eval-failure terminal such as the eval-day cap). WP17 is rewriting the rest of `EvalStateValue.ts` at the same time: re-read the file before every edit, change nothing else there, and keep your diff there to those lines. Tests for the new field go in `RenewalCycleObjective.test.ts` / `AverageRewardSolver.test.ts` (do not edit `EvalStateValue.test.ts`, WP17 owns it).
- **Not yours:** `Plan.ts`, `FeeSchedule.ts`, `core/Replacement.ts` (read them; reuse, do not duplicate), `FundedStateValue.ts`, `simulator/*`, CLI files. If `optimize dp` needs a wiring change, report it under needs_outside_change.

**Steps**

1. Read the WP14 handoff row in PLAN.md and D1 (cost per funded account = full expected spend: `initialEvalFee + (1/p - 1) * retryFee + subscription + activation once`, `retryFee = min(reset, rebuy)` via `FeeSchedule`, bundle `purchaseDiscounts`). Today `RenewalCycleObjective.entryCost` prices every cycle as a full re-buy (`feesUntilPass(0)`), with no retry path and no bundle.
2. Design: a cycle that ends in an eval failure is followed by a retry priced at the plan's retry fee (the same `retryFee` D1 uses, from `core/Replacement.ts` / `FeeSchedule`), not a fresh purchase. In the average-reward formulation, give the eval DP a failure terminal value `terminalValueAtFail = entryCost(rate) - retryCost(rate)` (the saving of a retry over a fresh start, both including the rebuy lag), add `RenewalCycleObjective.retryCost(ratePerDay)`, and wire it in `AverageRewardSolver`. A funded account that ends (bust or horizon) is still followed by a fresh purchase at `entryCost`. Apply `purchaseDiscounts` exactly as the simulator and D1 do (reuse the helper; if the bundle cannot be expressed per cycle, disclose it and pin the disclosure).
3. RED: a hand-computed toy where reset < eval fee: the average-reward rate with the retry path is strictly higher than today's and equals the hand value; a plan with `RetryKind.Rebuy` (retry = eval) gives exactly today's value (zero regression); `retryCost` unit tests; `terminalValueAtFail` default 0 leaves every existing EvalStateValue pin unchanged (run `EvalStateValue.test.ts` read-only).
4. GREEN, `bun run typecheck`, `bunx eslint <your files>`, guards `AverageRewardSolver.test.ts`, `RenewalCycleObjective.test.ts`, `EvalStateValue.test.ts` (read-only), `tests/unit/cli/prop/optimizeDp.test.ts` (read-only), and `bun run cli prop optimize dp --firm topstep --eval-days 20 --funded-days 60 --trials 200` plus one reset-cheaper-than-eval plan, reporting before/after figures from the CLI output.

**Acceptance**

- The renewal-cycle DP prices a failed eval at the D1 retry fee and a funded end at a fresh purchase; Rebuy plans are unchanged; the result agrees with D1's cost per funded account in a pinned toy.

## WP17e: DP solver convergence at long horizons (N-63 rest)

- **Wave:** 17  **Size:** large  **Depends on:** WP17c
- **Items:** N-63 (rest), plus the `252` literal in `optimize dp` and the `FundedDpPayoutCapGaps` rename (WP18k handoff)
- **Resume note (2026-09-25):** an earlier attempt of this package was stopped midway. Its partial work is in the working tree: new tests `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/FundedDpModelGaps.test.ts` (replacing the deleted `FundedDpPayoutCapGaps.test.ts`), the matching imports in `FundedReset.test.ts` and `optimizeDp.test.ts` (`fundedDpModelGapWarning`), which do not compile yet because the rename itself is not done, a measurement file `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/Wp17eProbe.test.ts` (delete it or turn it into a real test before you finish), and small edits in `FundedStateValue.ts`. Inspect them first (`git diff HEAD` and `git status`), then finish, correct or revert them; do not assume they are complete or correct, and do not count them as RED evidence without re-running them against the pre-change code.
- **Wave 17 coordination (WP17e, WP21a and WP21b run at once):** WP17e owns the DP core (`core/FundedStateValue.ts`, `core/EvalStateValue.ts`, `core/FundedCycleBaselineGrid.ts`, `core/AverageRewardSolver.ts`, `core/FundedDpPayoutCapGaps.ts` and its rename, `src/cli/commands/prop/optimize/dp/command.ts`). WP21a owns the engine files listed under it, WP21b the display/text files under it. Shared: `core/index.ts` (re-read before every edit, change only your own export lines) and test files that pin plan labels (WP21b changes labels; if a WP21a test file needs a label re-pin, WP21a makes that one-line change itself with a re-read first). Never revert another package's hunks.
- **Evidence:** WP17c's report (session scratchpad `wp17c.json`): at the CLI default grid `optimize dp` runs for hours (FTMO Futures Growth did not finish in 37 min; MFF Builder not in 60 min); with `meanHorizonDays` 252 the hazard value iteration contracts by about 0.996 per sweep, so `hazardConvergenceSweepCap` allows hundreds of sweeps per level; process memory is multi-GB (Builder 4.66 GB median, 5.17 GB peak).

**Steps**

1. Measure first on a quiet machine (check `ps` for stray `cli prop` or vitest processes and do not run solves concurrently): sweeps per level, wall time and peak memory for MFFU Rapid EOD, MFF Builder, FTMO Futures Growth, Apex EOD and Tradeify Growth at the coarse probe config, and one default-grid run with a hard time limit.
2. Cut the sweep count with a scheme that provably converges to the same fixed point: candidates are Gauss-Seidel ordering within a sweep, policy iteration (solve the fixed-policy linear system with a fast iterative method, few policy updates), successive over-relaxation, or extrapolation with MacQueen-style value-iteration error bounds as the stopping rule. The stopping tolerance and the error bound must be explicit and tested.
3. Parity rule: DP values must equal the current solver's to within the convergence tolerance (state the tolerance and the bound). Existing exact pins that move by less than the tolerance are re-pinned with the before/after values and the bound shown in the test; any larger move is a bug. Policies (sample risks) must stay identical unless a tie-break proof is given.
4. Memory: reduce the multi-GB footprint where it is safe (value and snapshot arrays, skeleton rows, per-solve scratch).
5. Replace `default: '252'` in `optimize dp` with `TRADING_DAYS_PER_YEAR`; rename `FundedDpPayoutCapGaps.ts` (and its kind enum/describer if the name says "payout cap") to a name that covers every funded-DP gap, updating imports, the barrel and tests.
6. Report before/after sweeps, wall time, states and peak memory per plan, and whether `optimize dp` at defaults is now practical; U14 (the best-day grid choice) stays with the user.
7. Run the DP test files (filter FundedStateValue.test.ts where you can), `bun run typecheck`, `bunx eslint <your files>`, `bunx knip`.

## WP39: bounded end-of-horizon credit, whole-contract percent sizing (N-71, N-72 credit half)

- **Wave:** 25 (next to WP41; prop-tools PT-05d and PT-11b are in flight on disjoint files)  **Size:** standard  **Depends on:** WP38b
- **Items:** N-71, N-72 (the credit half)
- **Decisions (binding, picked as the conservative defaults, user questions U17 and U18):** T32 (the credit is one capped request: ladder step, `payoutRequestSize`, `payoutProfitShare`, the no-ladder pool limit, `requestCap`, `balanceShareCap`; the day gate, funded consistency, minPayoutProfit and minPayoutRequest stay ignored as pinned) and T33 (percent-of-cushion needs `--stop-points`; whole contracts, at least one micro, at most the funded contract limit; one micro when it exceeds the affordable room; a flat dollar risk is also rounded down to whole contracts when `--stop-points` is given). `optimize funded` without `--stop-points`: an explicit `--percent` fails loud naming both flags; if the default candidate set contains percent rows, they are left out with a printed note instead of failing the command.
- **Live check first:** re-fetch Lucid's own help-center pages for the Pro and Direct 50K funded contract limits (engine: 4 minis / 40 micros, `LucidTrading.ts:34-39`) and quote them in `live-recheck.md` (append a dated section); if they differ or cannot be fetched, report it and do not change firm data.
- **Constraint:** keep every existing export's signature (the prop-tools advisor imports `resolveAffordableRisk`, `capRiskToRemainingDailyLoss`, `minStopPoints` and friends); `FundedStateValue.ts` calls `closeoutCredit` without a request size, so the new option stays optional and that file needs no edit.

- **Files owned:** `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/FundedPayoutCycle.ts`, `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/PositionSizing.ts`, `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/index.ts`, `src/lib/prop-calculator/simulator/day.ts`, `src/lib/prop-calculator/simulator/fundedPhase.ts`, `src/lib/prop-calculator/simulator/dayPolicyValidation.ts`, `src/cli/commands/prop/optimize/funded/command.ts`, `src/cli/commands/prop/shared.ts` (the --stop-points help text only), `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/FundedPayoutCycle.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/horizonCredit.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/PositionSizing.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/percentCushionSizing.test.ts` (new), `tests/unit/cli/prop/optimizeFunded.test.ts`

**Steps**

1. Run under ECC orch-fix-defect: ecc:tdd-guide drives RED/GREEN, ecc:typescript-reviewer reviews at the end. Edit only the owned files. No firm data changes. No code comments, use ~/ imports, and make no git commits (the user commits). FundedStateValue.ts calls closeoutCredit without a request size. Keep the new option optional so that file needs no edit.
2. RED, credit (FundedPayoutCycle.test.ts, new describe 'closeoutCredit per-request ceiling').
   - Lucid Pro no-DLL 50K, locked state, balance = payoutBalanceFloor + 1,000,000: payoutsIssued 0 gives payoutFromProfit(2000, 0) = 1800; payoutsIssued 1 and 5 (capsAtLastStep) give 2250. Today it returns about 900,000.
   - Lucid Direct 50K, same balance: payoutsIssued 0..2 give 1800, 3..6 give 2250.
   - Lucid Daily EOD with payoutRequestSize 500 and a large balance gives payoutFromProfit(500, n).
   - Lucid Flex (payoutProfitShare 0.5) gives payoutFromProfit(min(withdrawable, 0.5 * cycleProfit), n).
   - One Apex EOD case (deniesIfUnaffordable) with withdrawable below the step pins whatever resolveWithdrawal does: 0.
   - Keep every existing closeoutCredit pin unchanged: day gate, consistency, minPayoutProfit and minPayoutRequest ignored; 0 at lifetime caps and on an exhausted ladder; no mutation. Keep the toy-plan cases in horizonCredit.test.ts unchanged as a guard that the fix only adds caps.
3. RED, credit at engine level (horizonCredit.test.ts). Lucid Pro no-DLL, 25% cushion with MNQ stop 10, 2000 trials, seed 42, 252 days: expectedHorizonCredit <= fundedSurvivalProbability * payoutFromProfit(2500). Lucid Daily EOD, flat $1000, payoutRequestSize 500: credit per surviving account <= 0.9 * 500.
4. RED, sizing (PositionSizing.test.ts), new `wholeContractRisk(intendedRisk, positionSizing, maxContracts)`.
   - MNQ at 10 points ($20 a contract): 12.5 gives 20 (one micro); 59 gives 40; 1e12 with max 40 gives 800; max null gives max(1, floor(intended / 20)) * 20; max 0 gives 0.
   - NQ at 10 points ($200 a contract): 150 gives 200; 1e6 with max 4 gives 800.
5. RED, day and engine (new percentCushionSizing.test.ts, through the simulator's public API, no CLI).
   - A scripted all-loss rng at 25% cushion with MNQ stop 10 busts a funded Lucid Pro no-DLL account within a bounded number of trades.
   - 2000 trials, seed 42, commission 0: fundedBustProbability > 0 at 10%, 25% and 49%, and |bust(49%) - bust(50%)| stays within a sampling tolerance, which pins the float-tie artefact as gone.
   - Every trade's risk is an integer multiple of the contract risk, or equals the affordable room when one contract exceeds it and that room busts the account (the drawdown cushion or a terminating DLL); a lockout DLL room below one contract places nothing (WP39e, N-74).
   - dayPolicyValidation rejects fundedCushionPercent without position sizing, and the message names the missing stop.
6. RED, CLI (optimizeFunded.test.ts): `--percent 10` without `--stop-points` exits with an error that names both flags. With `--stop-points 10` it is accepted (instrument defaults to NQ). `--flat` alone is unaffected.
7. Confirm RED: `bunx vitest run tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/FundedPayoutCycle.test.ts tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/horizonCredit.test.ts tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/core/PositionSizing.test.ts tests/unit/lib/prop-calculator/percentCushionSizing.test.ts tests/unit/cli/prop/optimizeFunded.test.ts`. Record that the failures are the uncapped credit, fractional or zombie sizing, and silent acceptance, not unrelated errors.
8. Implement the credit.
   - Extract a private `perRequestCeiling({ deniesIfUnaffordable, ladderStep, payoutRequestSize, profitShareCap, withdrawable })` from resolveWithdrawal's ladder, request-size and profit-share arithmetic, and use it in both resolveWithdrawal and closeoutCredit (DRY). resolveWithdrawal keeps applying the no-ladder poolLimit and minRequest on top.
   - Add an optional `payoutRequestSize` to WithdrawableNowOptions and pass it from fundedPhase.ts:241.
   - closeoutCredit = payoutFromProfit(max(0, perRequestCeiling(...)), payoutsIssued). Consistency, the day gate, pool and minimums stay ignored unless U-N71-2 says otherwise.
9. Implement the sizing.
   - Add `wholeContractRisk` to PositionSizing.ts, export it via core/index.ts, and delete nothing else.
   - In day.ts, when the day policy is percent-of-cushion and positionSizing is non-null, replace the capRiskToContractLimit step with wholeContractRisk using the same contractLimitAt(...) limit. Then apply resolveTradeRisk as today. Flat risk keeps capRiskToContractLimit unless U-N71-4 says otherwise.
   - Win-side behaviour when one contract exceeds the affordable room follows U-N71-3. The default is to take one micro, with the loss capped at the cushion (MLL liquidation) and the win paying rr x one contract's risk. If that needs a rewardRisk separate from risk in runDay, keep it inside day.ts.
   - Add the percent-requires-position-sizing rule to dayPolicyValidation.ts. In optimize funded, reject a non-empty `--percent` without `--stop-points`, fail-loud, with no em dashes in the text. Update the --stop-points help to say it is required for --percent.
10. GREEN, then `bun run typecheck` and `bunx eslint <files>` (never `bun run lint`, which is `eslint . --fix`). Run these guards unchanged: engineCharacterization.test.ts, engineUniversalInvariants.test.ts, rebuyLag.test.ts, core/FundedStateValue.test.ts, cli/prop/optimizeDp.test.ts, core/ContractLimits.test.ts, and the firm suites lucidTrading, apex, tradeify, FtmoFutures. Re-pin a characterization number only with a traced reason, never loosen one.
11. CLI smoke (CLI only, no scripts, at most 2000 trials):
    - The N-71 repro on Lucid Pro no-DLL and Direct with `--stop-points 10 --instrument MNQ --percent 10,25,49,50`: bounded monthly, bust > 0 on every percent row, per-cycle and monthly on the same order of magnitude.
    - Record before and after for flat $1000 on Lucid Pro no-DLL (today per-cycle $2,077, monthly $6,609 at 2000 trials) and for Lucid Daily EOD flat $1000 at `--request-size 500` (the N-72 hand-off).
12. ## WP18f: live-engine follow-ups from WP18d's review (TopStep LFA capital, liquidation payout, DLL loss cap), barrels, notes, gate formatter

- **Wave:** 12b (runs alongside WP17/WP17b)  **Size:** medium  **Depends on:** WP18d
- **Items:** N-44 (rest), plus WP18d review leftovers R-3, R-4, R-5, R-7, R-8, R-10, R-12, and the "integration after wave 11 | me, after WP18d" handoff

- **Shared with WP17 (running):** `src/lib/prop-calculator/core/index.ts` (ADD export names only; re-read before each edit) and `src/lib/prop-calculator/simulator/types.ts` (ADD fields to `LiveOutputs` only, nothing else; re-read before each edit).
- **Evidence:** WP18d's report (scratchpad `wp18d.json` is a copy; the live quotes are in its N-44 notes): TopStep articles 8284233 (dateModified 2026-09-03) and 10657969 (dateModified 2026-09-17): "20% available to trade immediately", "80% held in Reserve, released in 4 increments of 25%", "Once 100% of your balance has been unlocked, you may withdraw those funds as well", "If your LFA balance drops below $1,000, the account may be immediately liquidated", and (live.md) "The remaining balance would then be sent as a final Payout." Re-fetch live if you rely on a quote not listed here.

**Steps**

1. R-5 capital-return channel: in `livePhase.ts`, split every withdrawal into the capital part (taken from below the running `state.startingBalance`: seed, or released Reserve once payable) and the profit part. Only the profit part goes into the recurring (annualized) withdrawals; report capital as a one-off, non-annualized figure next to `oneOffLiveCredit` (a new `LiveOutputs` field) and print it in `prop live` on its own line. RED first: TopStep `--request-size all` at 21 days annualizes the $9,000 seed today ($142,473/yr vs $14,089 at 252 days).
2. Reserve withdrawal after full unlock: once all four increments are released, the source allows withdrawing them ("Once 100% of your balance has been unlocked, you may withdraw those funds as well"). With step 1 in place, let `ReserveLivePlan` release them for withdrawal, reported as capital, never as income. Pin it.
3. R-12 final payout on liquidation: add `LivePlan.payoutOnLiquidation(state)` (0 by default); TopStep returns the remaining balance at an auto-liquidation bust ("The remaining balance would then be sent as a final Payout."), after the split rule the source states (or disclosed if it states none), reported as a one-off (capital part as capital). Not on inactivity closure unless a source says so. RED with a forced-bust test.
4. R-3 sizing: released Reserve IS tradable ("available to trade"), so remove WP18d's `cushionPercentFor` override (it makes the account stop trading once losses reach a released increment, then die of inactivity: the new stall). Instead fix the general engine flaw: no single trade may lose more than the remaining daily loss limit where the live plan has one (cap the risk in `LiveSizing`/`livePhase` using the plan's DLL inputs). Pin: no trade loses more than the active DLL at `--cushion-percent-pre-lock 10`, and the stall no longer happens (a run with losses into a released increment keeps trading).
5. Barrels (R-7): export `ReserveLivePlan` and its types from `core/index.ts`; switch `TopStepLive.ts` to the core barrel; re-export `findLiveTransitionPlanBuilder`, `LIVE_TRANSITION_PLAN_BUILDERS`, `type LiveTransitionPlanBuilder` and `LUCID_DAILY_LIVE_TRANSITION_PAYOUT_CAP` from the root barrel and switch `live/command.ts`; switch the remaining deep import in `tests/unit/cli/prop/live.test.ts` (`TRADING_DAYS_PER_YEAR`). Every export must have a barrel caller (knip).
6. Notes (R-4, R-10): rewrite the TopStep LFA note (`TopStep.ts` ~134-136) for the shipped model (static $1,000 floor, bust possible, seed payouts, Reserve mechanics, capital channel, liquidation payout, the unmodeled Friday DLL safeguard and discretionary denials, the 90/10 applied to seed as an assumption). Update the LucidDaily note in `LucidTrading.ts` (~197): `--transition-profit` now reaches the builder. Keep `TradingFirmNotes.test.ts` green.
7. R-8 DRY: one `formatGateCurrency(amount, { compact })` in `src/lib/format.ts` replacing `formatGateAmount` (plans/command.ts) and `formatPerCycleGate` (firstPayoutGate.ts). Remove the em dashes in RuleStressTestPanel's popover text.
8. Integration handoff: in `plans/command.ts` pass the rules to `describeShare(consistencyEval)` / `describeShare(consistencyFunded)` so `prop plans` shows Alpha's "40% (inclusive, fails on a net-losing cycle)"; then delete `describeShare`'s temporary `number` branch in `shared.ts`. Pin with plans.test.ts.
9. Run your tests, `bun run typecheck`, `bunx eslint <your files>`, `bunx knip`, and `bun run cli prop live --firm topstep` at default and `--request-size all` (report before/after p50 and annual rate).

**Acceptance**

- Capital (seed, Reserve, liquidation remainder) is never annualized as income; the Reserve is withdrawable only after full unlock; a liquidation pays the remaining balance; no trade loses more than the remaining DLL; no stall; barrels and notes clean.

## Coverage check (planner)

In-scope set: grep of audit_items.md gives 41 CONFIRMED R1 items, 4 DISPUTED R1 items (R1-15, R1-40, R1-48, R1-51) and TG-1..TG-8, 53 IDs total. The 9 rejected IDs (R1-5, R1-12, R1-16, R1-17, R1-20, R1-35, R1-38, R1-47, R1-52) are out of scope. Each in-scope ID sits in exactly one package.

Mapping:
R1-1 WP14 | R1-2 WP14 | R1-3 WP06 | R1-4 WP06 | R1-6 WP17 | R1-7 WP17 | R1-8 WP15 | R1-9 WP15 | R1-10 WP10 | R1-11 WP15 | R1-13 WP10 | R1-14 WP10 | R1-15 WP15 | R1-18 WP09 | R1-19 WP09 | R1-21 WP03 | R1-22 WP03 | R1-23 WP01 | R1-24 WP01 | R1-25 WP01 | R1-26 WP11 | R1-27 WP01 | R1-28 WP02 | R1-29 WP06 | R1-30 WP06 | R1-31 WP13 | R1-32 WP13 | R1-33 WP13 | R1-34 WP14 | R1-36 WP07 | R1-37 WP07 | R1-39 WP07 | R1-40 WP05 | R1-41 WP05 | R1-42 WP05 | R1-43 WP04 | R1-44 WP13 | R1-45 WP13 | R1-46 WP13 | R1-48 WP12 | R1-49 WP12 | R1-50 WP08 | R1-51 WP16 | R1-53 WP12 | R1-54 WP04 | TG-1 WP06 | TG-2 WP06 | TG-3 WP01 | TG-4 WP09 | TG-5 WP01 | TG-6 WP01 | TG-7 WP11 | TG-8 WP10.
Also covered, though not an audit ID: D3-plans-tag goes to WP14 with R1-34.

Steps that run in a package other than the one owning the ID (the ID keeps its single home):
- R1-23 header lines: run in WP06 (sim) and WP10 (ladder).
- R1-24 reader swaps: run in WP09 (live) and WP10 (ladder). Its sim cost/drawdown formatting runs in WP06. readNumber is deleted in WP11.
- R1-25 live --trials check: runs in WP09.
- R1-30 ladder 'eval pass' column: runs in WP15.
- DP half of R1-43 and R1-54: runs in WP17.

Where two architect designs conflicted, I picked one per CLAUDE.md's 'pick the more tested/recent pattern, don't blend' rule:
(1) D1 helper: engine-aggregates signature, replacementEconomics({discounts, evalPassRate, fees, meanDaysOnFail, meanDaysOnPass}). The ladder adapts to it.
(2) R1-31: PayoutCountTieredPayoutSplit plus payoutTiersFromPayout on a shared PayoutCountSchedule. Rejected: earlyPayoutShares.
(3) TierBasis naming: LiveProfit / PeakSessionCloseProfit / SessionOpenProfit, field tierBasis, context sessionOpenProfit. Rejected: PeakDayClose / SessionOpen / basis / profitAtSessionStart.
(4) DP peak handling: one exact ratchet dimension covering both contract and DLL tiers. Rejected: the separate R1-6 peak band, and the firm-data approach of deriving the peak from the threshold behind a guard.
(5) R1-51: MoveToLockedFloor with DrawdownStrategy.moveToLock / prospectiveLockThreshold. Rejected: MoveToPlanFloor with Plan.payoutReferenceThreshold.
(6) R1-36: margin-derived constants.
(7) Live flags use the generic WP01 readers. parseLiveSimInputs takes a LivePlanBuilder.
(8) One readLadderGrid, validated by the engine's ladderGridConfigSchema.
(9) TG-2's rankRows/compareOutputs is extracted in WP06; WP14 moves the cost sort to costPerFundedAccount.
(10) A single TradingInputs.toCouponDiscounts().
(11) describePayoutSplit and the drawdown-lock text live in plans/command.ts; the D3 label comes from core PLAN_AVAILABILITY_LABEL.
(12) MAX_INTRADAY_PATH_STEPS stays in day.ts.

File disjointness:
- Within every wave, no two packages share a file, including tests.
- core/index.ts (named-export barrel) is kept to one owner per wave: WP04, WP10, WP12, WP13, WP14, WP15, WP17. This is the main reason waves 4 to 8 each hold a single package.
